the map, and calls `RunMapSetupInitFunction` before the event/input loop. The named healing helper
at`0x23BFC` heals living allies and the hardcoded immortal exceptions, restores HP/MP, retains only
stun/poison/curse status bits and recalculates effective stats; it is not the already accepted
leader-only defeat recovery. Its complete roster/stat admission remains outside this request.

The map3 setup table names conditional609/506/543 variants. Even its default init at`0x51382`

prove the appropriate return-time setup/flag image or a fresh execution of those branches. A later
arrival slice must admit that state, preserve carried story/party facts, select the setup, define the
new operation/locomotion boundary and only then enable exploration. Step counter, non-admitted
allies/stat recomputation, setup flags/temporary flags, entity reload, original fade/music/VInt and
H4 remain explicit **Unknown** or unimplemented dependencies. No current claim covers them.

### Implemented fresh Granseal entry after Battle01 defeat

**Implemented as a controlled comparison.** One confirmation publishes one **fresh Map3 entry-ready
snapshot**, after the bounded semantic entry/setup/init work and before the first `WaitForEvent`.

claim of resumed walking, event dispatch or a continuous original playthrough. The subsequent slice
can admit the first movement/event operation against this new visit. Do not add a separate public
prepare/acknowledge phase merely to split this transaction.

but execute no follower or NPC actscript. Its positions are the source-supported declaration values
plus those explicit init effects, not an observation of original entity positions at the first
`WaitForEvent`. The original's intervening VInt/actscript execution remains outside this comparison.

Require exact profile agreement before and after; reject changed equipment/status/level or any profile outside that named comparison. Do not infer base values from effective stats; a general refresh needs separately admitted bases and item effects. Dormant7/28 use explicit neutral base/empty-item refresh. General status/equipment refresh is deferred. |

The first comparison therefore processes0/1/7/28; dead Chester2 and other ordinary dead slots skip.
Bowie remains HP12/MP8; Sarah is at her admitted maxima; Chester HP0 and his other stats remain
unchanged. The source-shaped loop must retain skipped rows exactly and expose its processed IDs,
before/after vitals and refresh policy. Include synthetic damaged-MP and nonzero-max immortal cases
in the owning tests; they must not quietly widen the production comparison.

`Battle01Stats` rejects maxHP0 and move0, so do not weaken it for dormant raw slots. A focused entry
slot representation in the new Domain entry owner is justified by those explicitly processed raw
slots and can reuse `Battle01Stats` for the three participating profiles. Do not introduce a general
inventory/party database, cache or second ongoing battle-stat store.

The Bowie-house roof definition retains its required `AppliedAfterWarp` identity. The existing
reader also exposes bounded Map3 return-load facts from the same parse: the exact referenced flag/chest tables, ordered10
roof rows, area1 foreground offset and selected record8, all under the existing pinned admission.
An immutable `OriginalMapReturnEntryLoadDefinition` in the new controlled-arrival input file holds
these facts and source identities; an optional property on `OriginalMapImportDefinition` carries
them. `OriginalMapRuntimeAdmission` owns central exact-profile validation. Startup validates
and binds that catalog-owned definition before `source.Admit`; missing, foreign or drifted facts
reject arrival selection. This adds no second JSON reader, schema, manifest, asset or generic roof
capability. Old callers need not select arrival, and the accepted house-warp definition stays intact.

Entry clones the pristine runtime layout, verifies the admitted F506/F220 no-op branches, and applies

words and the applied lifecycle in the completed arrival. The original load clears the copy latch
before this first application; duplicate entry cannot clear twice. Neither roof restore nor the

#### One current owner, fresh entities and atomic publication

Use a nullable completed `Arrival` facet on the existing `PrivateOriginalBattle01SessionSnapshot`,
following its accepted DefeatReturn publication pattern. The facet owns a typed immutable
`PrivateOriginalMapReturnArrivalSnapshot`: catalog-owned runtime3, a fresh working layout, area,
player position/facing, idle locomotion, fresh entity placements, current entry flag/counter values,
current party and the exact prior requested battle snapshot as history. It is a completed result,
not a new pending phase or acknowledgement queue. The current party has one owner, `Arrival.Party`;
the old Battle's HP/MP/gold/counters are immutable historical values after this handoff.

The focused `EnterPrivateOriginalBattle01Exploration(expectedSnapshot)` command must:

1. Require exact current requested snapshot, no prior Arrival, early arrival binding, unchanged
   preparation/source objects and the authenticated recovery/return. Validate all input/profile,
   setup, import, resource, population, destination and supported policy conditions first.
2. Build the party result, map-event/step-counter effects, map3/not-in-battle projection, fresh entity
   list, cleared temp image/F80, fresh layout, admitted flag/chest branches, area and church roof
   clear, then default-init effects in source order.

   provenance. Declare Sarah/Chester as followers from F66, omit their duplicate setup rows, include
   the player and other setup entities, then hide/move142 out as selected. Use a separate live
   placement overlay; never rewrite the immutable imported EntityPopulation or source snapshot.
3. Build idle locomotion at(32,13)/UP with an explicitly controlled counter0; reuse the existing
   direction/sheet/camera math via a typed entry relocation overload, never a fake cross-map receipt.
   Build the completed result and next session snapshot before **one** existing session assignment.
   A failure at any stage retains exact session, battle, request, party, map, history and projection.
4. Derive the host's current map/flow from Arrival when present (map3/Exploration, visibly entry-ready).
   Keep the complete requested battle,119 ordinary receipts, terminal/recovery before-images, RNG,
   source map40 and all palace/story objects reachable by their original references as history.
   Existing battle commands remain rejected; old first-visit exploration getters/mutators stay
   closed. Duplicate entry and all gameplay keys preserve the exact completed snapshot/projection.

This deliberately does not construct a false `PrivateOriginalMapSessionSnapshot`. Its constructor
requires the exact catalog runtime, active/traversable position and exactly one last operation for a
nonzero step, excludes completed history from step0, and limits palace/guard history to their admitted
route maps. Its Sarah/Zone601 lifecycle flags also encode the first visit. Clearing those objects,
resetting step0, inventing a traversal/warp receipt or broadening those invariants would lose facts
or enable the wrong event dispatcher. The new completed entry facet supplies a real new boundary
while the old objects remain frozen history. There is no second mutable current-map authority.

Fresh population validation must accept the **specific admitted same-cell group**: player0, Sarah1
and Chester2 each retain a distinct identity, current/target position(32,13), facing UP and their own
declaration/actscript provenance. The follower declarations retain zero velocity/travel while their
scripts remain unexecuted; the player uses `eas_Idle`. Preserve Chester's dead/BLUE_FLAME identity and
the skipped duplicate Sarah/Chester setup rows. Never import their old setup positions(42,8)/(44,10),
scatter them to empty cells, remove a follower, or merge identities to pass occupancy validation.
This exception is limited to those two authenticated F66 follower declarations with player0 at the
entry cell. A duplicate identity, extra/unadmitted follower, or genuinely blocking non-follower at
the player destination still rejects atomically under the existing population/destination rules;
it does not make arbitrary overlaps legal. No general collision or following engine is needed.

The existing Map3 presenter, base viewport and camera should gain small typed overloads delegating
to their shared runtime/layout/position projection logic. They must consume the real entry object;
do not manufacture a legacy snapshot for rendering or create another renderer/asset mount. Use the
already reviewed local Map3 atlas/player art and diagnostic fresh-entity glyphs, explicitly marking
dead Chester's blue-flame identity without claiming original flame art. Hide the battle canvas and
all first-visit interaction prompts. Show the entry-ready status with the bounded church-pocket
controls below. Party status distinguishes Chester's death from his follower declaration.
Display the declaration-position-freeze policy and list all three identities/positions in the
information panel if their same-cell glyphs overlap. Presentation must not change semantic positions
to make glyphs readable. Followers/NPCs remain fixed; the player uses the separate return movement below.

#### Entry facts and return-input ownership

Entry-ready is narrower than a full exploration return but includes the work that makes a new visit
real: party handoff/healing, setup selection, entity reset/init effects, current flags, map resources,
player placement and a visible fresh map. Original fade/music/VInt, sprite playback, general status
refresh, natural flags/party continuity and H4 remain **Unknown** or explicitly unimplemented.
The controlled stat policy and static diagnostic entities must remain visible limitations.
In particular, exact follower/NPC positions after original VInt/actscript execution and at the first
`WaitForEvent` are **Unknown**; the frozen same-cell declaration view does not close that boundary.

`ApplyPrivateOriginalMap` dispatches the first-visit Sarah/Zone601/messenger/door/warp lifecycle.
Running it against cleared temp flags and retained story would replay or suppress the wrong operations.
The [church-pocket movement](#implemented-return-visit-church-pocket-movement) therefore has its own
bounded return-input owner, carries `Arrival.Party` and fresh entity state, and reuses pure traversal
and locomotion. It leaves first-visit handlers closed without a general event framework or cache.

### Implemented return-visit church-pocket movement

**Implemented, bounded player-only.** The existing fresh-entry confirmation authenticates the
Arrival, early comparison inputs, default Map3 catalog, area1, party and source history. The player
can turn on terrain blocks and repeatedly traverse the pocket around the landing. Eligibility does
not require another preparation, acknowledgement or startup preset.

The interior pocket is `31 <= x <= 33`, `12 <= y <= 14`. Its only extension is the two-cell
[doorway cycle](#implemented-granseal-church-doorway-and-roof-cycle) below. This is a project-selected
capability boundary inside area1; it does not open general Granseal exploration or church services.

**Confirmed static data:** `Map03s1_Layout` at `0x984E8` has these main-layer words:

| y / x |31|32|33|
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
|12|`0x00DB`|`0xE8DC` (blocked)|`0x00DD`|
|13|`0x0061`|`0x0062` (entry group)|`0x0063`|
|14|`0x0076`|`0x0077`|`0x0078`|

The entry roof clear affects second-layer rows41..46 only. North from (32,13) is therefore a real
`word & 0xC000 == 0xC000` terrain block. The pocket has no matching step/roof/warp or specific
default-setup zone event, including wildcard coordinates; `Map3_DefaultZoneEvent` is a direct `rts`.
The priest's fixed declaration is entity141 at (32,11)/DOWN, outside the region.
The [canonical owner](../../docs/research/map-data-inventory.md),
[map/exploration contract](../../docs/design/contracts/map-exploration.md), existing
`uv run sf2 h2 map-import` / `tests/fixtures/h2/canonical-map-import-v1.json` and
`uv run sf2 h2 map-events` / `tests/fixtures/h2/map-events-static-v1.json` own those facts.
Static structure does not establish natural dispatcher reach.

Supported directional attempts increment one return-input ordinal and use pure traversal/locomotion.
Movement retains that same receipt through13 ticks; a terrain block changes facing/counter but keeps
position with the existing one-tick policy. The initial counter is the early controlled word0;
subsequent arithmetic follows the [locomotion evidence boundary](../../docs/research/map3-original-player-locomotion-animation.md#evidence-boundary).
It is distinct from the preserved first-visit SimulationStep, Battle receipt count and entry step-word
transform. No ongoing original random-battle or step-word continuity is claimed.

After departure, the frozen follower cell (32,13) remains typed Unsupported. Sarah and dead/BLUE_FLAME
Chester keep that declaration, UP facing and follower identity; NPCs and hidden/out entity142 retain
their exact records. F/G, side/outside candidates and old battle/first-visit APIs cannot mutate the
visit. Stale, foreign, null, busy and invalid requests preserve the complete current snapshot.

`EntryBeforeMovement` retains strict original declarations; only the player gets a live pose from
the current traversal/locomotion. Party, flags, all119 Battle receipts and preparation/source history
remain reference-identical. Door/roof changes are owned by the current layout/lifecycle described
below, with the original entry image kept immutable. `GameSession` constructs and validates a
successor before its sole publication. Godot consumes the same Arrival through its existing exclusive
input/physics route, camera and atlas projection. No legacy snapshot, additional renderer or mutable
map authority is introduced.

Owning executable checks are `PrivateOriginalBattle01ExplorationEntryTests`, the real required-private
`AcceptedExplorationEntryRetains119ReceiptsAndPublishesOneFreshFrozenVisit`, and the existing Godot
input/presenter/camera/viewport classes. The current [verification owner](./development-and-verification.md)
and [native recipe](./presentation-and-assets.md#diagnostic-battle01-launch-and-native-review) give the
single reproduction route. Natural follower/scripts, first WaitForEvent/VInt, general services,
original continuity, sound and H4 remain **Unknown** or Unsupported. New original-runtime research
still requires the three-part gate in [ADR0014](../../docs/decisions/0014-static-first-runtime-evidence-after-map3-battle01.md#admit-new-h3-only-through-a-three-part-gate-now)
and [ADR0016](../../docs/decisions/0016-remake-start-evidence-deferral.md#use-static-first-conditional-evidence-routing-now).

### Implemented Granseal church doorway and roof cycle

**Implemented, bounded player-only.** The current pocket adds only `(32,15)` and `(32,16)`, as a
union rather than a larger rectangle. The repeatable route is
`(32,14) -> (32,15) -> (32,16) -> (32,15) -> (32,14)`: the outside stop restores the roof and the
inside return keeps it cleared. The player can leave initial (32,13) southward or approach from
(31,13) through (31,14). The shared follower cell stays Unsupported after departure.

The owning Content test continues the actual119-receipt defeat/recovery/request/entry route through
this cycle. Native verification uses the same current snapshots for API and physical input comparison.
The behavior is a controlled remake continuation; original natural reach, follower positions and
VInt timing remain **Unknown**. It executes no original actscript or general church service.

#### Doorway data, layers and source order

**Confirmed static facts:** the byte-admitted canonical import uses USA ROM identity and upstream
commit pinned by `OriginalMapRuntimeAdmission`, including SF2DISASM
`c834c652b6862bc5679fd7f69a38a7093206efc6`. `Map03s1_Layout` is at `0x984E8`.
The following are main-layer cells; existing pocket cells and its north terrain block remain unchanged.

| Cell | Initial word | Relevant masked marker / role |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `(32,14)` | `0x0077` | Interior floor, neutral roof action |
| `(32,15)` | `0xC48F` | Collision plus `word & 0x3C00 == 0x0400`: door processing precedes passability |
| `(62,0)` | `0x080E` | Door-copy source; its `0x0800` marker is not collision |
| `(32,16)` | `0x0C57` | Outside floor, `0x0C00` restore marker |

`Map03s4_StepEvents` (`0x9786C`) has six rows. Its one-based row4 copies `(62,0)`, size1x1,
to `(32,15)` when that door target is checked. The target becomes `0x080E`: re-read that word
before checking warp, zone and collision. This is one main-layer word and remains open for this
visit; roof restoration must not close it or restore a pristine whole layout. A later pass through
`0x080E` no longer selects `OpenDoor` and creates no second door-copy receipt.

`Map03s5_RoofEvents` (`0x9789E`) has ten ordered rows. Its row8 has the same trigger `(32,15)`,
source255/255 and size5x6, but destination `(30,41)` is in the second-layer storage rows41..46.
Area1's second-layer foreground start is `(0,32)`, so that rectangle projects to world
`x30..34/y9..14`, not the doorway's main-layer coordinates. The initial `(32,13)` is inside it.
The existing entry load selects row8, saves its30 nonzero words, clears that rectangle and retains
an active lifecycle with ordinal8. This saved entry image, not a newly fabricated roof baseline,
is the starting state for the cycle. Door storage and roof storage do not overlap.

The [map/exploration contract](../../docs/design/contracts/map-exploration.md) and these pinned
source owners distinguish the two phases:

- `code/common/scripting/entity/entityscriptengine_2.asm`, `esc02_controlCharacter`: inspect the
  candidate marker in caravan/raft/door/warp/zone order, call `OpenDoor`, re-read the target word,
  then test passability. `code/gameflow/exploration/exploration.asm::OpenDoor` consumes the first
  exact step-table coordinate match and requests the existing plane update; its SFX is outside this slice.
  Its `CURRENT_BATTLE == 255` gate is already supplied by the current `Arrival.Party.CurrentBattle`
  entry result. Keep that current exploration owner distinct from the retained historical Battle;
  do not add another battle flag or bypass the original helper's mode restriction.
- `data/scripting/entity/eas_main.asm`, `byte_44E5A`: `ac_checkMapBlockCopy`, control, `ac_waitDest`,
  then loop. The `$40` action reads the block under the player, independently of the earlier door
  target check. `esc40_checkMapBlockCopy` skips while fading; masked `0x0800` calls
  `PerformMapBlockCopyScript`, and `0x0C00` calls `csub_40F2`.
- `exploration.asm::ToggleRoofOnMapLoad` chooses the first containing roof rectangle only on load.
  `PerformMapBlockCopyScript` instead scans trigger coordinates, preserves the one-based row ordinal
  in the busy state, and saves then copies/clears the rectangle. An already active state is a no-op.
  `csub_40F2` restores the saved rectangle and becomes inactive; an inactive restore is a no-op.
  Both actual mutations mark view channel0. The existing
  [bounded lifecycle fixture](../../tests/fixtures/h3/map-block-copy-lifecycle-v1.json) confirms these
  helper seams, including dispatcher `0x5D12`, perform `0x3FEA` and restore `0x40F2`; it does not
  establish natural-story reach or visible-frame timing. Reuse it without running new H3.

The complete step/roof tables have no other match in the proposed region, including wildcard
coordinates; the warp table has no match. The default setup's nine specific zone records, including
the x2/y255 row, also have no match, and `Map3_DefaultZoneEvent` is a direct `rts`.
No canonical NPC declaration occupies this region. The return overlay's two followers still occupy
only `(32,13)`; entity142 remains hidden/out. The accepted false F609/F506/F543 setup selection,
false F506 flag copies and false F220 chest condition remain fixed. No door/roof operation changes
party, story/temp flags, setup, area1, chest state or a warp/zone receipt. Outside the union, including
side cells at y15/y16 and `(32,17)`, refuse before any copy or terrain decision even when a floor is visible.

#### One live layout and a bounded settlement phase

The catalog-owned return load binds the exact church step row4 with `OriginalMapStepCopyDefinition`;
admission also requires C48F/080E/0C57 at the named cells. `RoofActions` projects all ten admitted rows
in source order, preserving ordinal8. Named startup presets and the four early input selections stay
unchanged.

`Arrival.WorkingLayout` is the sole current whole layout and `RoofLifecycle` is its current saved-region
state. `EntryBeforeMovement` and `RoofClear` retain the strict entry image and original saved30.
Validation compares all4096 words, admits only the door word and cleared/restored roof rectangle,
and rejects a foreign door binding, wrong saved words/ordinal/rectangle or unrelated map change.
The first `DoorCopy` receipt retains the exact entry, admitted definition, original directional input
and before/after word. It persists once; no separate door-open Boolean or whole-map reset is used.

Begin checks region/followers before copying, uses current `Arrival.Party.CurrentBattle == 255`,
re-reads the copied target marker and excludes unadmitted warp/zone/transport before traversal.
The existing13-tick locomotion continues without another input ordinal. The final moving Advance
calls `MapBlockCopyActionReducer` at the settled player cell. `LastRoofAction` records that input's
ordinal and result, preserving view-update marks and its original traversal receipt.

This is an explicit **controlled no-fade settlement policy** (`isFading:false`), not an observation
of original FADING_SETTING, VInt, WaitForEvent or action-cursor timing. Door art changes at Begin;
roof art changes at settlement. Original follower/NPC scripts remain unexecuted. Godot projects the
current typed Arrival with its existing camera/viewport and derives status from the door receipt and
live lifecycle. Frozen glyph coordinates and Chester's dead identity remain truthful; original
roof/sprite priority and occlusion are not claimed.

| Operation from a settled state | Begin / movement | Final settlement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| First south from `(32,14)` | Validate region/current state, copy door `C48F -> 080E`, re-read and traverse to `(32,15)`; roof stays clear/active8 through intermediate ticks | `0x0800` yields `ShowBusy`: preserve the exact saved30 and clear rectangle |
| South from `(32,15)` | Move to `(32,16)`; keep the prior roof until settlement | `0x0C00` yields `Restored`: restore exactly saved30 and become inactive; door remains `080E` |
| North from `(32,16)` | Traverse already-open `(32,15)` without another step copy; roof remains restored during motion | `0x0800` yields `Activated`: select row8, save the current30 roof words, clear them and become active8 |
| North from `(32,15)` | Move to `(32,14)` | Neutral: roof remains clear/active8; the next south/north cycle is available |

Each Begin/Advance constructs and validates layout, lifecycle, locomotion, pose and successor before
one session publication. A rejected call retains its exact before-image, including checked-ordinal
overflow and failure constructing the final restored-roof successor. It does not undo earlier accepted
calls, force settlement, mutate saved history or replay the door. Idle callbacks and extra Advances
after settlement run no roof action. Party, flags, all non-player records, complete119-receipt Battle
and source/preparation/recovery/request history stay unchanged.

#### Doorway owners and acceptance

Content admission lives in `OriginalBattle01ControlledArrivalInputs`, `OriginalMapRuntimeAdmission`
and `PrivateCanonicalMap3ImportReader`; `PrivateOriginalBattle01ExplorationEntry` and
`PrivateOriginalMapReturnMovement` own live state and publication. `PrivateMap3Presenter` projects
status. Existing Domain reducers, root input/physics and camera remain reused. The existing viewport
clips map drawing to its fixed logical rectangle, preserving partially visible diagnostics without
clamping their projected coordinates. Frozen follower glyphs still draw above the roof as a modern
diagnostic policy; original sprite/roof priority remains Unknown.

The required-private `AcceptedExplorationEntryRetains119ReceiptsAndPublishesOneFreshFrozenVisit`
compares all4096 words after Begin, intermediate/final Advance and refusals, exact saved30/active8,
one door receipt, repeated cycles and complete retained history. It covers malformed/foreign bindings,
wrong marker/layout/lifecycle, first-door overflow and a final-successor failure. The public synthetic
canonical sample contains only the minimum doorway facts plus authored zero block art; it does not
weaken byte-pinned private admission. Public unselected runs retain an explicit private-test skip.

`PrivateOriginalBattle01ExplorationEntryTests`, `PrivateCanonicalMap3ImportReaderTests`,
`MapBlockCopyActionTests` / `MapBlockCopyLifecycleTests` and the owning Godot classes cover their
respective boundaries. The native `leader-defeat-pending` probe retains its71-frame pocket prefix,
then captures first door Begin/middle/pre-settlement/settled, outside restore, re-entry clear,
two additional cycles and side/outside/follower/F/G/idle refusals. Actual uncovered door/roof pixels
must match the current projection and show changes at the selected phase; status text alone is
insufficient. The real door-copy middle frame also checks the partial actor141 glyph: no pixels may
escape the map's top edge, while its visible interior and original projected rectangle remain. The
probe checks the rendered background outside all four map edges on visible base views; the shared
multi-map review covers existing castle, tower and north-arrival consumers. The clipping mechanism
and before/after pixel comparison are described in the
[presentation owner](./presentation-and-assets.md#reproduce-the-bounded-native-image-review).
Every changed PNG needs inspection; only byte-identical accepted images are reusable.

Use the single current [verification route](./development-and-verification.md) and
[native recipe](./presentation-and-assets.md#diagnostic-battle01-launch-and-native-review), with fresh
ignored `local/granseal-doorway-cycle/` outputs. The committed planner selects public-core,
remake-dotnet and remake-godot. Run the selected full .NET suite once, preserve completed failures,
then rerun only failed or otherwise invalidated owning checks after correction. The seven-step
official gate and native probe consume exact committed archives. No H3/full Python or historical
environment repair is required. Natural continuity, followers/scripts, services/spells/revive,
broader events, original timing/presentation/audio and H4 remain **Unknown** or Unsupported.

#### Static doorway reproduction

The following optional static recipe records pinned data/source structure; it is not gameplay
verification or original runtime evidence. When its inputs or claims need rechecking, load the current
ignored environment and read-only canonical/upstream selections, save the code in a fresh ignored
`local/granseal-doorway-cycle/` directory and run `uv run python -X utf8 <ignored-run>/check.py`.
Save its JSON beside the script. Unchanged accepted static evidence need not be replayed for a
commit-only update. The current executable acceptance above owns actual session and visible behavior.

<!-- granseal-doorway-static-check:start -->
```python
import hashlib, json, os, re, subprocess
from pathlib import Path

repo = Path.cwd()
admission = (repo / 'remake/src/Sf2.Remake.Application/Content/OriginalMapRuntimeAdmission.cs').read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')
expected_digest = re.search(r'AcceptedContentDigest\s*=\s*"([A-F0-9]+)"', admission).group(1)
pin = 'c834c652b6862bc5679fd7f69a38a7093206efc6'
raw = Path(os.environ['SF2_PRIVATE_CANONICAL_MAP_IMPORT']).read_bytes()
assert hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest().upper() == expected_digest
data = json.loads(raw); assert data['upstream']['commit'] == pin
source = Path(os.environ['SF2_UPSTREAM_DISASM'])
assert subprocess.check_output(['git', '-C', str(source), 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], text=True).strip() == pin
named = ['code/gameflow/exploration/exploration.asm',
         'code/common/scripting/entity/entityscriptengine_2.asm', 'data/scripting/entity/eas_main.asm',
         'data/maps/entries/map03/4-step-events.asm', 'data/maps/entries/map03/5-roof-events.asm',
         'data/maps/entries/map03/mapsetups/s3_zoneevents.asm', 'data/maps/entries/map03/mapsetups/s1_entities.asm']
subprocess.run(['git', '-C', str(source), 'diff', '--exit-code', pin, '--', *named], check=True)
resources = data['resources']; map3 = next(m for m in data['maps'] if m['id'] == 3)
def resource(kind, identity): return next(r for r in resources[kind] if r['id'] == identity)
def table(kind, reference): return resource(kind, map3['references'][reference])['records']
layout = resource('layouts', map3['references']['layout']); words = layout['words']
assert layout['address'] == 0x984E8 and len(words) == 4096
region = {(x,y) for y in range(12,15) for x in range(31,34)} | {(32,15),(32,16)}
assert [words[y*64+x] for y in range(12,15) for x in range(31,34)] == [0xDB,0xE8DC,0xDD,0x61,0x62,0x63,0x76,0x77,0x78]
assert [words[15*64+32], words[62], words[16*64+32]] == [0xC48F,0x080E,0x0C57]
def matches(point): return any(point['x'] in (255,x) and point['y'] in (255,y) for x,y in region)
steps = table('stepEventTables','stepEventTable'); roofs = table('roofEventTables','roofEventTable')
assert len(steps) == 6 and [i+1 for i,r in enumerate(steps) if matches(r['trigger'])] == [4]
assert len(roofs) == 10 and [i+1 for i,r in enumerate(roofs) if matches(r['trigger'])] == [8]
assert steps[3] == dict(trigger=dict(x=32,y=15),source=dict(x=62,y=0),size=dict(width=1,height=1),destination=dict(x=32,y=15))
assert roofs[7] == dict(trigger=dict(x=32,y=15),source=dict(x=255,y=255),size=dict(width=5,height=6),destination=dict(x=30,y=41))
areas = table('areaTables','areaTable'); area = areas[0]
assert area['mainLayerStart'] == dict(x=0,y=0) and area['mainLayerEnd'] == dict(x=50,y=31)
assert area['secondLayerForegroundStart'] == dict(x=0,y=32)
assert [i+1 for i,r in enumerate(roofs) if r['destination']['x'] <= 32 < r['destination']['x']+r['size']['width'] and r['destination']['y']-32 <= 13 < r['destination']['y']-32+r['size']['height']] == [8]
assert not any(matches(r['trigger']) for r in table('warpEventTables','warpEventTable'))
zones = resource('zoneEventHandlers','ms_map3_ZoneEvents')['records']
assert len([r for r in zones if r['kind']=='specific']) == 9
assert not any(matches(r) for r in zones if r['kind']=='specific')
assert re.search(r'Map3_DefaultZoneEvent:\s+rts', (source/named[5]).read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
assert not any((e['x'],e['y']) in region for e in resource('entityLists','ms_map3_Entities')['records'])
assert {r['flag'] for r in table('flagEventTables','flagEventTable')} == {506}
assert table('itemTables','chestItemTable') == [dict(x=6,y=18,flag=220,item=127)]
engine = (source/named[1]).read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')
def ordered(text, needles):
    text=' '.join(text.split()); at=0
    for needle in needles: at=text.index(needle,at)+len(needle)
control=engine.split('esc02_controlCharacter:',1)[1].split('; End of function esc02_controlCharacter',1)[0]
ordered(control,['cmpi.w #$3800,d3','cmpi.w #$3C00,d3','cmpi.w #$400,d3','bsr.w OpenDoor','move.w (a4,d2.w),d3','andi.w #$3C00,d3','cmpi.w #$1000,d3','cmpi.w #$1400,d3','cmpi.w #$C000,(a4,d2.w)'])
action=engine.split('esc40_checkMapBlockCopy:',1)[1].split('; End of function esc40_checkMapBlockCopy',1)[0]
ordered(action,['tst.b ((FADING_SETTING-$1000000)).w','move.w (a0),d0','andi.w #$3C00,d2','cmpi.w #$800,d2','bsr.w PerformMapBlockCopyScript','cmpi.w #$C00,d2','bsr.w csub_40F2'])
ordered((source/named[2]).read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'),['byte_44E5A: ac_checkMapBlockCopy','ac_controlCharacter','ac_waitDest','dc.w (byte_44E5A-word_44E60)'])
indices=[y*64+x for y in range(41,47) for x in range(30,35)]; saved=[words[i] for i in indices]
assert len(saved)==30 and all(saved)  # area1 world rectangle x30..34/y9..14
entry=words.copy()
for i in indices: entry[i]=0
door=entry.copy(); door[15*64+32]=door[62]
outside=door.copy()
for i,v in zip(indices,saved): outside[i]=v
reentered=outside.copy()
for i in indices: reentered[i]=0
assert sum(a!=b for a,b in zip(entry,door))==1
assert sum(a!=b for a,b in zip(door,outside))==30 and reentered==door
assert all(outside[i]==words[i] for i in range(4096) if i!=15*64+32)
assert door[15*64+32]&0x3C00==0x800 and outside[16*64+32]&0x3C00==0xC00
print(json.dumps(dict(status='Pass',boundary='Static data/source inspection; no gameplay or original timing execution',
    upstreamCommit=pin,layout=layout['id'],doorWords=['C48F','080E'],outsideWord='0C57',
    stepOrdinal=4,roofOrdinal=8,savedWords=30,roofWorldBounds=[30,9,34,14],
    noOtherRegionEventsOrDeclaredNpcs=True,doorAndRoofStorageDisjoint=True,
    firstDoorChangedWords=1,roofChangedWords=30,staticReentryMatchesOpenInterior=True),indent=2))
```
<!-- granseal-doorway-static-check:end -->

### Controlled Godot Battle01 consumer

The existing private profile parser accepts three explicit paths, together:
`--private-battle01-data`, `--private-battle01-scene`, and `--private-battle01-terrain`, each in
`--name=value` form. Relative, empty, malformed, duplicate and partial selections are unavailable;
public/default profiles cannot consume them. The UI carries paths only. The existing Content reader
performs file/identity/semantic admission at N, so missing files or rejected payloads display the
Prepare stage while retaining Pending. No selected inputs means the existing Pending endpoint remains.

At the actual Map40 Pending, N invokes Prepare, Initialize, FirstRound and FirstControl in order with
the named accepted comparison inputs. Each endpoint receives the exact current object and actual
current candidate; there is no actor1 constant, reroll, extra RNG call or reorder. Preparation or
initialization rejection keeps Map40/Pending. Initialization closes exploration: later round/control
failure displays the actual battle phase and diagnostic instead of returning to Map40 or retrying init.

The view explicitly distinguishes reviewed fixed Map57 base art plus diagnostic units from the
unrequested-art diagnostic mode. `--private-map3-base-atlas` plus selected Battle01 inputs requires
the current accepted Map57 asset; failure is visible before startup. Its projection reads the current
battle's `Preparation.Pending.Definition`, preserves its fixed layout/blocks/area/selection and rejects
any used slot3/4 reference. The two unused seed blocks do not become drawable empty art. The shared
block sampler renders the full 16-by-20 area at 24 pixels per block, preserving the 2x/4x physical
raster on a 384-by-480 logical map. It executes no original layer, scroll, VRAM or animation pipeline.
The right column holds status and controls; live units, range, legal stops, current actor, cursor/path
and costs/budget share the same 24-pixel grid. Units remain project-authored markers. Terrain is shown
at the current cursor; without requested art, authored cells also show terrain IDs.
I/J/K/L request adjacent cursor destinations; out-of-area and unreachable steps preserve state with
an explanation. Space provisionally relocates only a legal stop. At `PlayerActionChoice`, a separate
Space press commits STAY, while A opens manual physical targeting. I/J cycle backward and K/L
forward; target Space confirms Attack and target Backspace returns to the provisional action choice.
A second Backspace restores movement origin; repeated origin cancellation remains a rejection.
After a completed action the UI tries
the actual next candidate once and displays its active range when supported. At an unsupported
candidate it shows the completed actor and candidate/reason with old range/path/cursor cleared.
The same finite relay generates later rounds and admits bounded inactive standby, active pursuit
and ordinary physical attacks. The controlled attack132 applies damage once and yields actual
Bowie0 control with HP9 and a persistent result. His explicit origin attack132 adds EXP15 and
persists enemy HP2; actual131/133 then lead to round7 player2 control with that result still visible.
Manual player2 STAY and actual131/132/133 yield Bowie HP6. His next origin Attack132 completes
the first defeat, retains the source row while removing its live placement, and yields actual
player1 movement/cancel with EXP39/gold60/kills1 and the persistent reward result.
The selected continuation moves Chester to(11,14) in R8 while Bowie stays at(11,15); actual131
hits Chester11→9, then R9 returns his movement/cancel control. The newest enemy result replaces
the older reward message while all prior accounting and the unplaced132 row persist. With the
new named Chester EXP0 preparation, his origin Attack131 creates receipt72/EXP10, then actual
128/129/131/133 return Sarah movement/cancel at receipt76 with Bowie HP3 and main25991234/copy0634.
The old null-EXP preparation still rejects Chester attacks; his kills remain unspecified in both.
Sarah origin STAY and actual130 return Bowie at receipt78. His origin target131 at terrain0 takes4
damage and dies once, adding24 EXP/60 gold/one kill. The same production relay generates R10 from
seven survivors and yields Chester move/confirm(12,14)/cancel, retaining EXP63/gold120/kills2,
Chester HP9/EXP10, both unplaced corpses and four enemy markers. This is the current stopping point.
Other combat profiles/terrain, spells, items, validated follow-ups, required level changes,
third defeats/Chester kills/victory and original cancellation/death animation remain unsupported.

Root input and physics route on current battle authority before any closed exploration getter.
Entering battle hides all old exploration/HUD/synthetic canvas subtrees. Fresh process/session
startup remains Map3. The existing native probe's narrow mode uses an explicitly controlled Map40
seed, then actual Godot physical-key events for the Pending route and battle operations; this is
**Confirmed** bounded native controllability when its assertions, screenshots and process receipt pass.
Natural Map3 continuity, original Map57 presentation and H4 remain **Unknown**. Reproduction and
visual acceptance belong to [presentation and assets](./presentation-and-assets.md#diagnostic-battle01-launch-and-native-review).

### Ordered path to the first controllable Battle 01 turn

1. Consume the independently accepted pending boundary above as the sole continuation input.
   Its receipt is neither a completed warp receipt nor an active battle snapshot.
2. **Input preparation and controlled initialization implemented.** Consume the fixed definition
   and explicit comparison preset above through the existing Content trust boundary:
   [placement](../../docs/research/battle01-placement.md),
   [spritesets](../../docs/research/battle-spriteset-data.md),
   [terrain/routing](../../docs/research/battle-routing-data.md), and
   [global data](../../docs/research/battle-global-data.md). Reuse maintained selected-battle outputs;
   no whole canonical re-extraction. The accepted preparation fixes controlled party
   stats/status/equipment/spells and RNG inputs. Account for the accepted before/start semantic effects in the
   new-battle order while explicitly skipping original scene, text, music and fade presentation; do
   not claim original programs executed. Keep intro/start F451 separate from F401 unlock and F501 completion.
   Atomically consume pending into a battle-owned Map 57 state only when all required inputs validate;
   failure retains pending. Roster and terrain/occupancy now belong to the initialized battle.
3. **Controlled first-round generation, first-player movement and diagnostic Godot consumer implemented.**
   The thin consumer connects the exact current control/selection state to a visible grid and input using the
   [control contract](../../docs/design/contracts/battle-functions-control-flow.md) and
   [navigation contract](../../docs/design/contracts/battlefield-navigation.md): expose actor/turn and
   terrain-aware movement/selection with cancel only for player control. First-control entry still
   rejects an AI-first or unsupported candidate; the separate first-enemy consumer requires both
   completed player turns. Never skip or reshuffle turn order. The accepted bridge-seeded player-ready H3 case is
   a bounded comparison input, not a naturally carried save or a universal “actor 1 first” rule.
   The API has deterministic semantic movement tests and the diagnostic consumer has a bounded native
   input/capture recipe. No-effect STAY closes the first turn and dispatches the next supported player
   once. After that player's movement/cancel/STAY, all six actual inactive enemies complete bounded
   standby/no-effect STAY turns through the finite relay above. Actual Bowie0 then receives Regular1
   control and independent move/cancel/STAY. The first sentinel at offset18 feeds repeatable wholly
   inactive rounds from current state. Actual candidates dispatch until manual player control or a
   precise retained rejection; the comparison reaches round3 control with eighteen receipts.
   Known activated GIZMOs continue through the bounded pursuit and physical consumers above;
   actual attack132 yields HP9 Bowie0 control. Broader combat, death and victory remain later slices.
   The fixed Map57 atlas candidate now uses the palette8/unloaded-slot proof and controlled storage
   policy above. Its accepted local asset now feeds the fixed-layout Godot base composition;
   original scene/layers/VRAM/animation fidelity remains separate acceptance work.
   Clearly labeled diagnostic combatant markers may expose the control seam before original art is
   admitted; that is not complete private presentation or an original-fidelity result.

Each later item needs its own exact ownership and acceptance anchor. Missing concrete startup inputs
block that item, not the pending boundary; natural continuity and H4 stay at their accepted owners
under [ADR 0016](../../docs/decisions/0016-remake-start-evidence-deferral.md), without an automatic
emulator-work queue.

## Decision Rules

- Prefer the next player-visible capability and reuse the existing Domain, Application, Content, and
  thin Godot boundaries before adding abstractions.
- Keep original facts evidence-owned. Label authored composition, cadence, scaling, and usability
  choices as modern remake policy rather than original behavior.
- Use actual reviewed game art or music only through the local zero-remote asset repository; do not
  synthesize substitutes. Raster presentation may use nearest or supersampled 2x/4x high-DPI output,
  and HUD chrome may use reviewed SVG reconstruction where it preserves the established style.
- Keep private assets out of the main repository and public PCK by default. A local/private success
  does not authorize redistribution or an original-fidelity claim.
- Do not bypass an evidence gap with a guessed route, hidden fallback, or diagnostic behavior
  relabelled as gameplay.


#### Reproduce terminal acceptance

Use the same registered read-only inputs and required-private switch. Run the new owning Content
method above plus the `leader-defeat-pending` native recipe in
[Presentation and Assets](./presentation-and-assets.md#diagnostic-battle01-launch-and-native-review).
Use a fresh `local/leader-defeat-pending` root for this slice; prior diagnostic/source/native and
root-review outputs remain read-only. To reuse the source-only recipe above there, set
`SF2_ROUND14_OUTPUT` to the new root and change its two explicit root assertions to that same path.
Copy its existing `start.json` and `boundary.json` comparison inputs into the new root, or regenerate
the old-preset diagnostic there. Redirect only the earlier reducer's output expression as shown;
never change the source arithmetic, seed it from a terminal capture, or overwrite prior results.
Compare the resulting `sourceDerivedOnly` against the fresh native receipt only after reduction.
Naturally carried Bowie defeats remains Unknown; the new preparation separately supplies0->1.
